#!/bin/sh
# roblox-external-update-001 — mirror payload wave 001: full first-party source
# snapshot of the roblox external (the update-delivery baseline).
#
# Built per mirror/xbps-fetch-mirror-template.txt §13:
#   self-contained — the source snapshot rides inside this file (base64 gzip tar),
#                    so a paste host expiring mid-install can never half-apply it;
#   sha-gated      — the embedded archive sha AND a per-file manifest are verified
#                    before anything is written and again after extraction;
#   fail-closed    — refuses an existing target dir (never overwrites), aborts on
#                    any mismatch, touches nothing outside the dir you pass.
#
# Carries: roblox_external first-party sources + roblox_external.slnx (see
# SOURCE_COMMIT below). NOT carried, target keeps its own copies: vendored
# roblox_external/imgui/ (ImGui 1.91.1 backends) and glass-obsidian/.
# Build after extract: VS2022, toolset v143 (see repo README), Release|x64.
#
# Usage (run from a file, never from a pipe):
#   sh roblox-external-update-001.txt /absolute/extract/dir
# The extract dir must NOT exist yet. PASS = the line UPDATE001_DONE=YES.
# INVERSE (full undo): rm -r /absolute/extract/dir — nothing else is touched.
set -u
UPDATE=001
SOURCE_COMMIT="af980fd af980fdc063302e4d893d4d9885481ca994f412d"
ARCHIVE_SHA256=1b2ca07264d22dd520f64df4ce88d283e813a137b5e3243e8cddcedf4dd72686
FILES=73
[ -f "$0" ] || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=run-from-a-file-not-a-pipe (fetch it to a file first)"; exit 1; }
[ $# -eq 1 ] || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=usage: sh roblox-external-update-001.txt /absolute/extract/dir"; exit 1; }
DEST=$1
case "$DEST" in
  /*|[A-Za-z]:/*) ;;
  *) echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=absolute-path-required got:$DEST"; exit 1 ;;
esac
if [ -e "$DEST" ]; then
  echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=target-already-exists:$DEST (never overwrite; rm -r it first or pass a new dir)"
  exit 1
fi
for c in base64 tar sha256sum mktemp; do
  command -v "$c" >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=missing-tool:$c"; exit 1; }
done
TMP=$(mktemp -d) || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=mktemp"; exit 1; }
b64d() { base64 -d "$1" 2>/dev/null || base64 -D "$1" 2>/dev/null; }
sed -n '/^__UPDATE001_PAYLOAD__$/,$p' "$0" | tail -n +2 > "$TMP/b64.txt"
b64d "$TMP/b64.txt" > "$TMP/src.tar.gz" 2>/dev/null || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=base64-decode"; exit 1; }
GOT=$(sha256sum "$TMP/src.tar.gz" | cut -d' ' -f1)
echo "ARCHIVE_SHA256_GOT=$GOT"
echo "ARCHIVE_SHA256_WANT=$ARCHIVE_SHA256"
if [ "$GOT" != "$ARCHIVE_SHA256" ]; then
  echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=archive-sha-mismatch (nothing was written; stop and quote both shas)"
  exit 1
fi
mkdir -p "$DEST" || { echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=cannot-create:$DEST"; exit 1; }
if ! tar -xzf "$TMP/src.tar.gz" -C "$DEST"; then
  echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=tar-extract-errored (partial output may sit at $DEST; inverse: rm -r $DEST)"
  exit 1
fi
FAIL=0
N=0
while read -r want path; do
  [ -n "$want" ] || continue
  N=$((N+1))
  if [ ! -f "$DEST/$path" ]; then
    echo "FAIL $path MISSING"
    FAIL=1
    continue
  fi
  got=$(sha256sum "$DEST/$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)
  if [ "$got" = "$want" ]; then
    echo "PASS $path $got"
  else
    echo "FAIL $path got=$got want=$want"
    FAIL=1
  fi
done <<'__UPDATE001_MANIFEST__'

__UPDATE001_MANIFEST__
rm -rf "$TMP"
echo "UPDATE=001"
echo "SOURCE_COMMIT=$SOURCE_COMMIT"
echo "EXTRACT_DIR=$DEST"
echo "FILES_IN_MANIFEST=$FILES FILES_VERIFIED=$N"
echo "NOTE=vendored roblox_external/imgui/ and glass-obsidian/ are NOT carried; keep the target's own copies"
echo "BACKUP=NONE (fresh extraction only; no pre-existing file was touched)"
echo "INVERSE=rm -r $DEST"
if [ "$FAIL" = "0" ] && [ "$N" = "$FILES" ]; then
  echo "UPDATE001_DONE=YES"
  exit 0
fi
echo "UPDATE001_FAIL=manifest-verification-failed (inverse: rm -r $DEST)"
exit 1
__UPDATE001_PAYLOAD__
